  Mia: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Quinn last night. Quinn is a Villager. With that confirmed, we need to focus on others. I think we should look into Tara and Leo — their behavior has been suspicious. Let's discuss and vote wisely.
  Noah: I think Tara's behavior is too calm, like she's hiding something. Also, Leo kept avoiding eye contact during the night. We should consider them as possible werewolves and vote them out.
  Bob: I think Noah and Mia are jumping to conclusions. Leo and I are allies, and we’ve been working together to keep the group safe. If you keep pointing fingers without proof, you’ll end up eliminating your own. Let’s think logically before voting.

While Tara’s behavior is suspicious, we should avoid assumptions. Let’s focus on logical deductions and not let fear drive our decisions.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on Tara based on Iris's confirmation that Leo is safe. While Leo's behavior was odd, the Doctor's action clears him. Tara’s calmness and lack of clear alignment make her a stronger suspect. Let’s vote her out.
  Zoe: As a Werewolf, I believe we need to stay calm and avoid drawing attention to ourselves. Tara is a strong suspect, and voting her out would help us eliminate a potential threat without raising suspicion. I support the idea of targeting Tara.

While Tara’s behavior is suspicious, we should avoid assumptions. Let’s focus on logical deductions and not let fear drive our decisions.
  Paul: I think we need to focus on Tara based on Iris's confirmation that Leo is safe. While Leo's behavior was odd, the Doctor's action clears him. Tara’s calmness and lack of clear alignment make her a stronger suspect. Let’s vote her out.

  Jack: I think we should focus on Tara based on the lack of clear alignment and the Doctor's confirmation that Leo is safe. While Leo's behavior was odd, Iris's action suggests he's not a werewolf. Tara’s calmness and suspicious behavior make her the most logical target. Let’s vote her out.
